   1++6E44              ; ============================================================================
   2++6E44              ; Project127 ATM Core — linked kernel image layout measurement
   3++6E44              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++6E44              ;
   5++6E44              ; Measures the actual assembled Stage2 kernel span [p127_entry,p127_end).
   6++6E44              ; It is not a fixed reservation: the linked end label determines the size.
   7++6E44              ; The current loader contract places the image at 6000h and bounds it by A000h.
   8++6E44              ; Both live host pages used by that CPU window (P5 and P2) must be RESERVED.
   9++6E44              ; This module validates placement only; it does not relocate or map pages.
  10++6E44              ; ============================================================================
  11++6E44              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++6E44              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  13++6E44              P127_LAYOUT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  14++6E44              P127_LAYOUT_START         EQU 06000h
  15++6E44              P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT         EQU 0A000h
  16++6E44              P127_LAYOUT_MAX_BYTES     EQU P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT-P127_LAYOUT_START
  17++6E44
  18++6E44              p127_layout_selftest:
  19++6E44                      ; The linked entry must match the BASIC loader's CODE address.
  20++6E44 21 00 60             ld      hl,p127_entry
  21++6E47 11 00 60             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_START
  22++6E4A B7                   or      a
  23++6E4B ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  24++6E4D C2 8C 6E             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  25++6E50
  26++6E50                      ; Require a non-empty image and an end no higher than the UI window.
  27++6E50 21 88 93             ld      hl,p127_end
  28++6E53 11 00 60             ld      de,p127_entry
  29++6E56 B7                   or      a
  30++6E57 ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  31++6E59 DA 8C 6E             jp      c,p127_layout_reject
  32++6E5C CA 8C 6E             jp      z,p127_layout_reject
  33++6E5F 22 A6 6E             ld      (p127_kernel_size),hl
  34++6E62 7C                   ld      a,h
  35++6E63 FE 41                cp      041h
  36++6E65 D2 8C 6E             jp      nc,p127_layout_reject
  37++6E68
  38++6E68 21 88 93             ld      hl,p127_end
  39++6E6B 11 00 A0             ld      de,P127_LAYOUT_LIMIT
  40++6E6E B7                   or      a
  41++6E6F ED 52                sbc     hl,de
  42++6E71 DA 7A 6E             jp      c,p127_layout_end_ok
  43++6E74 CA 7A 6E             jp      z,p127_layout_end_ok
  44++6E77 C3 8C 6E             jp      p127_layout_reject
  45++6E7A              p127_layout_end_ok:
  46++6E7A                      ; P5 backs the #6000..#7FFF portion; P2 backs #8000..#9FFF.
  47++6E7A 3A 35 77             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+5)
  48++6E7D FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  49++6E7F C2 8C 6E             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  50++6E82 3A 32 77             ld      a,(p127_mem_page_state+2)
  51++6E85 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
  52++6E87 C2 8C 6E             jp      nz,p127_layout_reject
  53++6E8A B7                   or      a
  54++6E8B C9                   ret
  55++6E8C
  56++6E8C              p127_layout_reject:
  57++6E8C 37                   scf
  58++6E8D C9                   ret
  59++6E8E
  60++6E8E              ; Report the measured byte count in hexadecimal, high byte first.
  61++6E8E              p127_layout_report:

  63++6E91 CD 06 63             call    p127_puts
  64++6E94 3A A7 6E             ld      a,(p127_kernel_size+1)

  67++6E9D CD 22 63             call    p127_put_hex8

  70++6EA6

 451+ 6EE0                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_resident.asm"
# file opened: p127_resident.asm
   1++6EE0              ; ============================================================================
   2++6EE0              ; Project127 — resident-window contract / software preflight
   3++6EE0              ; Module version: 0.4.0
   4++6EE0              ; Defines the only first-stage remap candidate without touching hardware.
   5++6EE0              ; W1/P5 owns resident execution+data; W2/P2 owns resident stack/control.
   6++6EE0              ; W3 is the only candidate target and may be used only after UI quiescence.
   7++6EE0              ; W0 is ROM. No OUT instruction is permitted here.
   8++6EE0              ; ============================================================================
   9++6EE0              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  10++6EE0              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  11++6EE0              P127_RESIDENT_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  12++6EE0
  13++6EE0              ; The remap-critical resident module is deliberately placed first after the
  14++6EE0              ; Stage2 boot body. W1/P5 is the fixed execution/data window; W2/P2 is the
  15++6EE0              ; fixed stack window; W3 is the only target. This preflight checks the linked
  16++6EE0              ; addresses and current live stack at runtime. It performs no page OUT.
  17++6EE0              p127_resident_start:
  18++6EE0
  19++6EE0              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW  EQU 1
  20++6EE0              P127_RESIDENT_CODE_PAGE    EQU 5
  21++6EE0              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW EQU 2
  22++6EE0              P127_RESIDENT_STACK_PAGE   EQU 2
  23++6EE0              P127_RESIDENT_TARGET       EQU 3
  24++6EE0              P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED  EQU 1
  25++6EE0
  26++6EE0              ; IN: A=target window, B=resident code window, C=resident stack window,
  27++6EE0              ;     D=flags (bit 0 set means UI is quiesced).
  28++6EE0              ; OUT: carry=0 if the fixed resident contract is satisfied; carry=1 otherwise.
  29++6EE0              ; This is a software contract check, not a physical mapping operation.
  30++6EE0              p127_resident_validate:
  31++6EE0 32 6A 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_target),a
  32++6EE3 78                   ld      a,b
  33++6EE4 32 6B 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_code),a
  34++6EE7 79                   ld      a,c
  35++6EE8 32 6C 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_stack),a
  36++6EEB 7A                   ld      a,d
  37++6EEC 32 6D 6F             ld      (p127_resident_arg_flags),a
  38++6EEF
  39++6EEF 3A 6A 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  40++6EF2 FE 03                cp      P127_RESIDENT_TARGET
  41++6EF4 C2 1E 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  42++6EF7 3A 6B 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_code)
  43++6EFA FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  44++6EFC C2 1E 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  45++6EFF 3A 6C 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_stack)
  46++6F02 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  47++6F04 C2 1E 6F             jp      nz,p127_resident_reject
  48++6F07 3A 6D 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_flags)
  49++6F0A E6 01                and     P127_RESIDENT_UI_QUIESCED
  50++6F0C CA 1E 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  51++6F0F                      ; Explicitly assert target is distinct from both resident windows.
  52++6F0F 3A 6A 6F             ld      a,(p127_resident_arg_target)
  53++6F12 FE 01                cp      P127_RESIDENT_CODE_WINDOW
  54++6F14 CA 1E 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  55++6F17 FE 02                cp      P127_RESIDENT_STACK_WINDOW
  56++6F19 CA 1E 6F             jp      z,p127_resident_reject
  57++6F1C B7                   or      a
  58++6F1D C9                   ret
  59++6F1E
  60++6F1E              p127_resident_reject:
  61++6F1E 37                   scf
  62++6F1F C9                   ret
  63++6F20
  64++6F20              ; Synthetic software-only tests: accepted W3 with UI quiesced; reject W1,
  65++6F20              ; reject W2, reject active UI, and reject a displaced resident code window.
  66++6F20              p127_resident_selftest:
  67++6F20 3E 03                ld      a,3
  68++6F22 06 01                ld      b,1
  69++6F24 0E 02                ld      c,2
  70++6F26 16 01                ld      d,1
  71++6F28 CD E0 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  72++6F2B DA 68 6F             jp      c,p127_resident_test_fail
  73++6F2E
  74++6F2E 3E 01                ld      a,1
  75++6F30 06 01                ld      b,1
  76++6F32 0E 02                ld      c,2
  77++6F34 16 01                ld      d,1
  78++6F36 CD E0 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  79++6F39 D2 68 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  80++6F3C
  81++6F3C 3E 02                ld      a,2
  82++6F3E 06 01                ld      b,1
  83++6F40 0E 02                ld      c,2
  84++6F42 16 01                ld      d,1
  85++6F44 CD E0 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  86++6F47 D2 68 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  87++6F4A
  88++6F4A 3E 03                ld      a,3
  89++6F4C 06 01                ld      b,1
  90++6F4E 0E 02                ld      c,2
  91++6F50 16 00                ld      d,0
  92++6F52 CD E0 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
  93++6F55 D2 68 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail
  94++6F58
  95++6F58 3E 03                ld      a,3
  96++6F5A 06 00                ld      b,0
  97++6F5C 0E 02                ld      c,2
  98++6F5E 16 01                ld      d,1
  99++6F60 CD E0 6E             call    p127_resident_validate
 100++6F63 D2 68 6F             jp      nc,p127_resident_test_fail

 349++7111              ; Stage 2.10 — real-address resident trampoline preflight (software only).
 350++7111              ; Requires DI on entry; DI is asserted again and interrupts remain disabled.
 351++7111              ; Z80 does not provide a general instruction to read/restore prior IFF1, so
 352++7111              ; this entry contract intentionally does not attempt an EI/IFF round trip.
 353++7111              ; W1 is #4000-#7FFF; W2 is #8000-#BFFF. The linked resident module must fit
 354++7111              ; wholly in W1, all resident state is inside that module, and the live stack
 355++7111              ; must be in W2. No page register is written.

   1++7160              ; ============================================================================
   2++7160              ; Project127 — interrupt safety contract / preflight
   3++7160              ; Module version: 0.5.0
   4++7160              ; This is deliberately a DI-only bootstrap contract, not an IRQ dispatcher.
   5++7160              ; No EI, IM instruction, vector write, or hardware page OUT is permitted.
   6++7160              ; ============================================================================
   7++7160              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   8++7160              P127_IRQ_VERSION_MINOR EQU 5
   9++7160              P127_IRQ_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  10++7160              P127_IRQ_MASKED EQU 1
  11++7160              P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE EQU 0
  12++7160              P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET EQU 0
  13++7160
  14++7160              ; Boot owns the CPU in a DI-protected phase. A stock Z80 cannot read IM0/1/2
  15++7160              ; directly; LD A,I exposes IFF2 in P/V but is not a general IFF restore path.
  16++7160              ; Therefore this module never guesses prior interrupt state and never EI/IM.
  17++7160              ; Until a real vector table/handler is installed, vector ownership is NONE.
  18++7160              ; Interrupts may not be enabled until all three prerequisites are explicitly
  19++7160              ; established by later stages: writable/owned vector table, verified table
  20++7160              ; mapping, and installed resident handler. No current boot code sets these.
  21++7160              P127_IRQ_READY_VECTOR EQU 1
  22++7160              P127_IRQ_READY_MAP EQU 2
  23++7160              P127_IRQ_READY_HANDLER EQU 4
  24++7160              P127_IRQ_READY_ALL EQU 7
  25++7160
  26++7160              p127_irq_init:
  27++7160 F3                   di
  28++7161 3E 01                ld      a,P127_IRQ_MASKED
  29++7163 32 42 74             ld      (p127_irq_mask_state),a
  30++7166 AF                   xor     a
  31++7167 32 43 74             ld      (p127_irq_vector_owner),a
  32++716A 32 44 74             ld      (p127_irq_mode_policy),a
  33++716D 32 45 74             ld      (p127_irq_ready_flags),a
  34++7170 B7                   or      a
  35++7171 C9                   ret
  36++7172
  37++7172              ; OUT: carry clear only while the documented DI-only/no-vector contract holds.
  38++7172              p127_irq_validate:

  40++7175 FE 01                cp      P127_IRQ_MASKED

  42++717A 3A 43 74             ld      a,(p127_irq_vector_owner)
  43++717D FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_VECTOR_NONE
  44++717F C2 C9 72             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  45++7182 3A 44 74             ld      a,(p127_irq_mode_policy)
  46++7185 FE 00                cp      P127_IRQ_MODE_UNSET
  47++7187 C2 C9 72             jp      nz,p127_irq_reject
  48++718A B7                   or      a
  49++718B C9                   ret

 237++72D0              ; Stage 2.15: resident interrupt frame protocol.
 238++72D0              ; Hardware vector installation and IM selection are deliberately separate.
 239++72D0              ; Entry contract: CPU has already pushed the interrupted PC; W1/P5 code and
 240++72D0              ; W2/P2 stack remain mapped. The dispatcher may freely clobber all registers.
 241++72D0              ; RETI restores IFF1 from IFF2; this routine never executes EI or IM.

 245++72D2 D5                   push    de

 248++72D6 FD E5                push    iy
 249++72D8 D9                   exx
 250++72D9 C5                   push    bc
 251++72DA D5                   push    de

   1++7446              ; ============================================================================
   2++7446              ; Project127 ATM Core — physical page-state manager
   3++7446              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4++7446              ; ABI: reserve A=first page, B=count; alloc -> A=page, C=exhausted;
   5++7446              ;      free A=page, C=invalid/not allocated.
   6++7446              ; Model: 64 physical 16 KiB page IDs = exactly 1 MiB.
   7++7446              ; ATM Turbo 2+ page IDs are physical 16 KiB RAM pages. Bootstrap reservations
   8++7446              ; reflect the active ZX-128 map: page 5 (window 1), page 2 (window 2), and
   9++7446              ; page 0 (window 3). Page 5 is already covered by the video-page union.
  10++7446              ; ============================================================================
  11++7446              P127_MEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  12++7446              P127_MEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  13++7446              P127_MEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  14++7446              P127_MEM_PAGE_COUNT    EQU 64

  16++7446              P127_MEM_FREE          EQU 0
  17++7446              P127_MEM_RESERVED      EQU 1

   2++77B0              ; Project127 ATM Core — UINT32 physical range validator
   3++77B0              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++77B0              ; ABI:
   5++77B0              ;   p127_range_start  = 4-byte little-endian physical start address
   6++77B0              ;   p127_range_length = 4-byte little-endian byte length
   7++77B0              ;   call p127_range_validate; carry=1 if invalid, clear if valid; clobbers AF, B, DE, HL

 455+ 78CA                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_arena.asm"
# file opened: p127_arena.asm
   1++78CA              ; ============================================================================
   2++78CA              ; Project127 ATM Core — top-down physical program-page arena
   3++78CA              ; Module version: 0.1.2
   4++78CA              ;
   5++78CA              ; Arena discovers the highest contiguous FREE physical run from the page-state
   6++78CA              ; map. The current reservation map yields P8..P63; P6 remains isolated below
   7++78CA              ; reserved P7. Physical pages are 16 KiB and P63 ends at 0x100000.
   8++78CA              ;
   9++78CA              ; API:
  10++78CA              ;   p127_arena_init: discovers and validates the highest contiguous FREE run.
  11++78CA              ;   p127_arena_alloc: IN B=page count; OUT A=first page, carry=1 on failure.
  12++78CA              ;                    Allocates downward from the current high boundary.
  13++78CA              ;   p127_arena_free: IN A=first page, B=count; only most-recent block accepted.
  14++78CA              ;                   Frees it and contracts the high boundary upward.
  15++78CA              ;
  16++78CA              ; This is page-granular accounting only. It does not map pages, access RAM,
  17++78CA              ; provide byte-granular allocation, or promise a seamless legacy 16-bit ABI.
  18++78CA              ; Free is intentionally LIFO until per-process ownership/coalescing exists.
  19++78CA              ; ============================================================================
  20++78CA              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  21++78CA              P127_ARENA_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  22++78CA              P127_ARENA_VERSION_PATCH EQU 2
  23++78CA              P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE      EQU 64
  24++78CA              P127_ARENA_PAGE_SIZE      EQU 04000h
  25++78CA
  26++78CA              ; Discover the highest contiguous FREE run from the actual page-state map.
  27++78CA              ; The first RESERVED/ALLOCATED page below that run is the lower guard.
  28++78CA              ; An all-FREE map is rejected: required system reservations are missing.
  29++78CA              p127_arena_init:
  30++78CA 21 6F 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state+P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  31++78CD 0E 3F                ld      c,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE-1
  32++78CF 06 40                ld      b,P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE
  33++78D1              .p127_arena_init_scan:
  34++78D1 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
  35++78D2 B7                   or      a
  36++78D3 C2 DD 78             jp      nz,.p127_arena_init_guard_found
  37++78D6 2B                   dec     hl
  38++78D7 0D                   dec     c
  39++78D8 10 F7                djnz    .p127_arena_init_scan
  40++78DA C3 90 79             jp      p127_arena_reject
  41++78DD              .p127_arena_init_guard_found:
  42++78DD 0C                   inc     c
  43++78DE 79                   ld      a,c
  44++78DF FE 40                cp      P127_ARENA_HIGH_PAGE

   1++7A28              ; ============================================================================
   2++7A28              ; Project127 ATM Core — managed logical address-space page table
   3++7A28              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7A28              ;
   5++7A28              ; 4 MiB logical space: 256 logical pages x 16 KiB. A logical page maps to one
   6++7A28              ; allocator-owned physical ATM page (0..63). Translation metadata only:
   7++7A28              ; no CPU-window mapping, physical byte access, IN/OUT or DMA.
   8++7A28              ;
   9++7A28              ; API:
  10++7A28              ;   p127_as_init: clear all slots to FFh (unmapped).
  11++7A28              ;   p127_as_bind: A=logical page 0..255, B=allocated physical page 0..63.
  12++7A28              ;   p127_as_unbind: A=logical page; removes metadata, does not free RAM.
  13++7A28              ;   p127_as_translate: p127_as_address (uint32 LE) -> A=physical page,
  14++7A28              ;                      HL=14-bit page offset; carry=1 if invalid/unmapped.
  15++7A28              ; Caller must unbind before releasing a physical page.
  16++7A28              ; ============================================================================
  17++7A28              P127_AS_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  18++7A28              P127_AS_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  19++7A28              P127_AS_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0

  21++7A28              P127_AS_PAGE_SIZE EQU 04000h

   5++7CC9              ; Metadata only: no byte access/copy, mapping, OUT, DMA, IRQ, IM or EI.

   1++7EA9              ; ============================================================================
   2++7EA9              ; Project127 — managed logical-span copy
   3++7EA9              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++7EA9              ;
   5++7EA9              ; Copies between non-overlapping mapped logical spans using the guarded W3
   6++7EA9              ; lease and a resident 64-byte bounce buffer. W1/P5 holds this code/buffer;
   7++7EA9              ; W2/P2 holds the live stack. No UI call occurs while W3 is leased.
   8++7EA9              ;
   9++7EA9              ; API inputs:
  10++7EA9              ;   p127_as_copy_source      uint32 little-endian
  11++7EA9              ;   p127_as_copy_destination uint32 little-endian
  12++7EA9              ;   p127_as_copy_length      uint32 little-endian
  13++7EA9              ; Outputs:
  14++7EA9              ;   carry clear = success; carry set = failure
  15++7EA9              ;   p127_as_copy_completed   exact bytes written before return
  16++7EA9              ;   p127_as_copy_error       0 success, 1 span, 2 overlap, 3 lease,
  17++7EA9              ;                            4 source map, 5 destination map, 6 lease restore
  18++7EA9              ; Overlap is rejected; this is not memmove. Unexpected runtime failures after
  19++7EA9              ; writes begin may leave a destination prefix modified.
  20++7EA9              ; ============================================================================
  21++7EA9              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  22++7EA9              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  23++7EA9              P127_AS_COPY_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  24++7EA9              P127_AS_COPY_BUFFER_SIZE EQU 64
  25++7EA9              P127_AS_COPY_WINDOW_BASE EQU 0C000h
  26++7EA9
  27++7EA9              p127_as_copy:
  28++7EA9 AF                   xor     a
  29++7EAA 32 A8 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_error),a
  30++7EAD 32 A4 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed),a
  31++7EB0 32 A5 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+1),a
  32++7EB3 32 A6 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+2),a
  33++7EB6 32 A7 83             ld      (p127_as_copy_completed+3),a
  34++7EB9
  35++7EB9                      ; Validate source and destination completely before taking W3.
  36++7EB9 21 98 83             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_source
  37++7EBC 11 95 7E             ld      de,p127_as_span_start
  38++7EBF 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  39++7EC2 ED B0                ldir
  40++7EC4 21 A0 83             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_length
  41++7EC7 11 99 7E             ld      de,p127_as_span_length
  42++7ECA 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  43++7ECD ED B0                ldir
  44++7ECF CD C9 7C             call    p127_as_span_check
  45++7ED2 DA BB 80             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject
  46++7ED5
  47++7ED5 21 9C 83             ld      hl,p127_as_copy_destination
  48++7ED8 11 95 7E             ld      de,p127_as_span_start
  49++7EDB 01 04 00             ld      bc,4
  50++7EDE ED B0                ldir
  51++7EE0 CD C9 7C             call    p127_as_span_check
  52++7EE3 DA BB 80             jp      c,p127_as_copy_span_reject
  53++7EE6
  54++7EE6                      ; Empty spans are valid no-ops, including an address exactly 4 MiB.
  55++7EE6 3A A0 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length)
  56++7EE9 47                   ld      b,a
  57++7EEA 3A A1 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+1)
  58++7EED B0                   or      b
  59++7EEE 47                   ld      b,a
  60++7EEF 3A A2 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+2)
  61++7EF2 B0                   or      b
  62++7EF3 47                   ld      b,a
  63++7EF4 3A A3 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length+3)
  64++7EF7 B0                   or      b
  65++7EF8 CA C9 80             jp      z,p127_as_copy_success
  66++7EFB
  67++7EFB                      ; Calculate both end-exclusive addresses (preflight proved no overflow).
  68++7EFB 2A 98 83             ld      hl,(p127_as_copy_source)
  69++7EFE 3A A0 83             ld      a,(p127_as_copy_length)
  70++7F01 4F                   ld      c,a

 177++8557              ; ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 178++8557              ; Safe-remap preparation gate (software-only).
 179++8557              ; IN: A=target window 0..3, B=new physical page 0..63,
 180++8557              ;     C=bitmask of protected windows (bit 0=W0 ... bit 3=W3).
 181++8557              ; OUT: carry=1 if invalid/protected; carry=0 if a pending request is staged.
 182++8557              ; This routine NEVER writes a page port and NEVER changes the active map.
 183++8557              ; Caller must include execution, stack, UI, interrupt and host/staging windows
 184++8557              ; in C. The bootstrap currently protects all four windows (#0F).
 185++8557              p127_map_handoff_prepare:
 186++8557                      ; Keep the target in E, invalidate any older staged request, then
 187++8557                      ; validate the page/window and index the four-byte mask with DE=0:E.
 188++8557 5F                   ld      e,a
 189++8558 AF                   xor     a
 190++8559 32 18 86             ld      (p127_map_pending_valid),a
 191++855C 78                   ld      a,b
 192++855D FE 40                cp      64
 193++855F D2 1B 86             jp      nc,p127_map_handoff_reject
 194++8562 7B                   ld      a,e
 195++8563 FE 04                cp      P127_MAP_WINDOW_COUNT
 196++8565 D2 1B 86             jp      nc,p127_map_handoff_reject
 197++8568 16 00                ld      d,0
 198++856A 21 14 86             ld      hl,p127_map_window_mask
 199++856D 19                   add     hl,de
 200++856E 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 201++856F A1                   and     c
 202++8570 C2 1B 86             jp      nz,p127_map_handoff_reject
 203++8573 7B                   ld      a,e
 204++8574 32 19 86             ld      (p127_map_pending_window),a
 205++8577 78                   ld      a,b

   2++874D              ; Project127 — resident stack handoff probe
   3++874D              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++874D              ; Runs from the resident Stage2 image in W1/P5. Scratch stack is a bounded
   5++874D              ; test area in the still-mapped W2/P2. No page port is written.

  12++874D              ; between its low-water boundary and the scratch stack.

  14++874D              P127_HANDOFF_STACK_BOTTOM  EQU 0B800h

  17++874D              P127_HANDOFF_GUARD_HI      EQU 0B7FFh
  18++874D
  19++874D              ; IN: none.
  20++874D              ; OUT: carry=1 on failure; carry=0 on success.
  21++874D              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL, IX and IY. AF/flags are return status.
  22++874D              ; Requires interrupts disabled. DI is asserted defensively and this routine
  23++874D              ; returns with interrupts disabled. IM and interrupt vectors are untouched.
  24++874D              ; The active stack must be at/above #B900 after this routine's save frame.
  25++874D              ; This is a stack-integrity probe only: W2 remains mapped throughout.

# file opened: p127_speaker.asm
   1++8D35              ; ============================================================================
   2++8D35              ; Project127 ATM Turbo 2+ beeper output driver
   3++8D35              ; Module version: 0.1.0
   4++8D35              ;
   5++8D35              ; Hardware: Spectrum/ATM #FE, D4 = speaker, D3 = tape output,
   6++8D35              ; D0..D2 = border colour. OUT (#FE),A also presents A3 from A's bit 3
   7++8D35              ; on ATM, so the caller-supplied D3 state is preserved.
   8++8D35              ;
   9++8D35              ; This is a low-level output latch only. It does not invent a timer,
  10++8D35              ; enable interrupts, block the main loop, or emulate an IBM PC speaker.
  11++8D35              ; The Z80 host owns this physical port. NeoGS must request sound through
  12++8D35              ; the host transport/DMA and must never issue physical port I/O.
  13++8D35              ;
  14++8D35              ; API:
  15++8D35              ;   p127_speaker_init  IN A = initial #FE low nibble (border + tape)
  16++8D35              ;   p127_speaker_set   IN A bit 0 = speaker level (0/1)
  17++8D35              ;   p127_speaker_toggle
  18++8D35              ;   p127_speaker_stop
  19++8D35              ;   p127_speaker_get   OUT A = shadow latch
  20++8D35              ;
  21++8D35              ; Caller must initialize with the actual current border/tape state before
  22++8D35              ; using set/toggle/stop. No safe hardware readback of the #FE output latch
  23++8D35              ; is assumed.
  24++8D35              ; ============================================================================
  25++8D35              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
  26++8D35              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_MINOR EQU 1
  27++8D35              P127_SPEAKER_VERSION_PATCH EQU 0
  28++8D35
  29++8D35              P127_SPEAKER_PORT EQU 0FEh
  30++8D35              P127_SPEAKER_MASK EQU 010h
  31++8D35              P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK EQU 00Fh
  32++8D35
  33++8D35              p127_speaker_init:
  34++8D35 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  35++8D37 32 93 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  36++8D3A C9                   ret
  37++8D3B
  38++8D3B              ; Set D4 while preserving border D0..D2 and tape D3.
  39++8D3B              ; OUT (#FE),A intentionally uses A3 as part of the ATM border/BRIGHT
  40++8D3B              ; address decode; preserving D3 also preserves that address-line state.
  41++8D3B              p127_speaker_set:
  42++8D3B C5                   push    bc
  43++8D3C 47                   ld      b,a
  44++8D3D 3A 93 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  45++8D40 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  46++8D42 4F                   ld      c,a
  47++8D43 78                   ld      a,b
  48++8D44 E6 01                and     1
  49++8D46 28 05                jr      z,p127_speaker_set_low
  50++8D48 79                   ld      a,c
  51++8D49 F6 10                or      P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  52++8D4B 18 01                jr      p127_speaker_write
  53++8D4D              p127_speaker_set_low:
  54++8D4D 79                   ld      a,c
  55++8D4E              p127_speaker_write:
  56++8D4E 32 93 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  57++8D51 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  58++8D53 C1                   pop     bc
  59++8D54 C9                   ret
  60++8D55
  61++8D55              ; Invert only D4. Border/tape state remains unchanged.
  62++8D55              p127_speaker_toggle:
  63++8D55 3A 93 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  64++8D58 EE 10                xor     P127_SPEAKER_MASK
  65++8D5A 32 93 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  66++8D5D D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  67++8D5F C9                   ret
  68++8D60
  69++8D60              ; Force speaker low without changing D0..D3.
  70++8D60              p127_speaker_stop:
  71++8D60 3A 93 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  72++8D63 E6 EF                and     0EFh
  73++8D65 32 93 8D             ld      (p127_speaker_shadow),a
  74++8D68 D3 FE                out     (P127_SPEAKER_PORT),a
  75++8D6A C9                   ret
  76++8D6B
  77++8D6B              p127_speaker_get:
  78++8D6B 3A 93 8D             ld      a,(p127_speaker_shadow)
  79++8D6E C9                   ret
  80++8D6F
  81++8D6F
  82++8D6F              ; Audible hardware smoke test. Approximately 0.57 s at 7 MHz or 1.14 s at
  83++8D6F              ; 3.5 MHz; pitch therefore depends on the active ATM CPU clock. This is
  84++8D6F              ; intentionally a one-shot diagnostic, not the asynchronous sound service.
  85++8D6F              ; A=low #FE nibble to preserve (caller must supply the live border/tape state).
  86++8D6F              p127_speaker_test_tone:
  87++8D6F F5                   push    af
  88++8D70 C5                   push    bc
  89++8D71 D5                   push    de
  90++8D72 E5                   push    hl
  91++8D73 E6 0F                and     P127_SPEAKER_LOW_MASK
  92++8D75 CD 35 8D             call    p127_speaker_init
  93++8D78 11 58 02             ld      de,0258h
  94++8D7B              p127_speaker_test_loop:
  95++8D7B CD 55 8D             call    p127_speaker_toggle
  96++8D7E 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
  97++8D80              p127_speaker_test_delay1:
  98++8D80 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay1
  99++8D82 06 FF                ld      b,0FFh
 100++8D84              p127_speaker_test_delay2:
 101++8D84 10 FE                djnz    p127_speaker_test_delay2
 102++8D86 1B                   dec     de
 103++8D87 7A                   ld      a,d
 104++8D88 B3                   or      e
 105++8D89 20 F0                jr      nz,p127_speaker_test_loop
 106++8D8B CD 60 8D             call    p127_speaker_stop
 107++8D8E E1                   pop     hl
 108++8D8F D1                   pop     de
 109++8D90 C1                   pop     bc
 110++8D91 F1                   pop     af
 111++8D92 C9                   ret
 112++8D93
 113++8D93 00           p127_speaker_shadow: db 0
 114++8D94

  17+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_WIDTH EQU 80
  18+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_HEIGHT EQU 25
  19+ A000              P126_ATM_TEXT_FRAME_SIZE EQU 4000
  20+ A000              P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT EQU #00EF
  21+ A000
  22+ A000 06           p126_atm_video_mode: db P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
  23+ A001 00           p126_atm_video_enabled: db 0
  24+ A002 00           Console_X: db 0
  25+ A003 0C           Console_Y: db BIOS_DEBUG_TOP
  26+ A004 00           bios_dbg_row: db 0
  27+ A005 00           bios_dbg_col: db 0
  28+ A006 00           bios_dbg_char: db 0
  29+ A007 00           bios_dbg_write_row: db 0
  30+ A008 00           bios_dbg_write_col: db 0
  31+ A009 00           bios_dbg_parity: db 0
  32+ A00A 00           bios_dbg_attr: db 0
  33+ A00B 00 00        bios_dbg_offset: dw 0
  34+ A00D 00 00        bios_dbg_text_ptr: dw 0
  35+ A00F 00           bios_dbg_print_row: db 0
  36+ A010 00           bios_dbg_print_col: db 0
  37+ A011
  38+ A011              p126_atm_video_init:
  38+ A011
  39+ A011 F5                   push    af
  40+ A012 C5                   push    bc
  41+ A013 D5                   push    de
  42+ A014 E5                   push    hl
  43+ A015
  44+ A015                      ; Reset the ATM paging/control latch BEFORE enabling permanent
  45+ A015                      ; DOSEN. On this board #EFF7 is part of the ATM paging control path;
  46+ A015                      ; resetting it after the #0177 shadow-port setup can undo the state
  47+ A015                      ; needed by the TR-DOS overlay.
  48+ A015 AF                   xor     a
  49+ A016 01 F7 EF             ld      bc, #EFF7
  50+ A019 ED 79                out     (c), a
  51+ A01B
  52+ A01B                      ; ROM2=1 (D4) is required for TR-DOS overlay; D3 selects alternate
  53+ A01B                      ; video pages 7/3. This is #18, matching the host display setup.
  54+ A01B 3E 18                ld      a, #18
  55+ A01D 01 FD 7F             ld      bc, #7FFD
  56+ A020 ED 79                out     (c), a
  57+ A022 32 3B 5C             ld      (#5C3B), a
  58+ A025
  59+ A025                      ; Open shadow ports and hold DOSEN permanently (A9=0) using the
  60+ A025                      ; documented TR-DOS ROM trampoline. Do this AFTER the paging reset.
  61+ A025 01 35 A0             ld      bc, p126_atm_ports_open_done
  62+ A028 C5                   push    bc
  63+ A029 01 53 2A             ld      bc, #2A53
  64+ A02C C5                   push    bc
  65+ A02D 01 77 01             ld      bc, #0177
  66+ A030 3E C3                ld      a, #C3
  67+ A032 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
  68+ A035
  69+ A035              p126_atm_ports_open_done:
  70+ A035                      ; Window 0 must use ROM with D7=1 so its ROM page selection is
  71+ A035                      ; coupled to ROM2/DOSEN. #BF selects the low ROM page with the
  72+ A035                      ; DOSEN-controlled low ROM select: BASIC-48 when DOSEN=0, TR-DOS
  73+ A035                      ; when DOSEN=1. This is the ATM2+ documented auto-TR-DOS mapping.
  74+ A035 3E BF                ld      a, #BF
  75+ A037 01 F7 3F             ld      bc, #3FF7
  76+ A03A ED 79                out     (c), a
  77+ A03C
  78+ A03C
  79+ A03C                      ; Map the alternate screen pages while the 32-byte mode trampoline
  80+ A03C                      ; is installed at #FFEC. Window 3 uses a fixed RAM page here; the
  81+ A03C                      ; TR-DOS auto-select mux is configured separately in window 0.
  82+ A03C 3E 78                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE7
  83+ A03E 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
  84+ A041 ED 79                out     (c), a
  85+ A043 3E 7C                ld      a, P126_VIDEO_PAGE3
  86+ A045 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
  87+ A048 ED 79                out     (c), a
  88+ A04A
  89+ A04A                      ; LD BC,#0177 / OUT (C),A / JP p126_atm_video_mode_ready
  90+ A04A 21 EC FF             ld      hl, #FFEC
  91+ A04D 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  92+ A04F 23                   inc     hl
  93+ A050 36 77                ld      (hl), #77
  94+ A052 23                   inc     hl
  95+ A053 36 01                ld      (hl), #01
  96+ A055 23                   inc     hl
  97+ A056 36 ED                ld      (hl), #ED
  98+ A058 23                   inc     hl
  99+ A059 36 79                ld      (hl), #79
 100+ A05B 23                   inc     hl
 101+ A05C 36 C3                ld      (hl), #C3
 102+ A05E 23                   inc     hl
 103+ A05F 36 6E                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready & #FF
 104+ A061 23                   inc     hl
 105+ A062 36 A0                ld      (hl), p126_atm_video_mode_ready >> 8
 106+ A064
 107+ A064                      ; ATM Turbo 2+ RG=6: hardware text 80x25.
 108+ A064 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 109+ A066 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 110+ A069 3E C6                ld      a, #C6
 111+ A06B C3 EC FF             jp      #FFEC
 112+ A06E
 113+ A06E              p126_atm_video_mode_ready:
 114+ A06E                      ; Restore CPU-visible host/staging pages. Keep D7 clear on #FFF7 so
 115+ A06E                      ; window 3 remains fixed to RAM page 0 for the TR-DOS staging buffer.
 116+ A06E 3E 7A                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE5
 117+ A070 01 F7 7F             ld      bc, #7FF7
 118+ A073 ED 79                out     (c), a
 119+ A075 3E 7F                ld      a, P126_HOST_PAGE0
 120+ A077 01 F7 FF             ld      bc, #FFF7
 121+ A07A ED 79                out     (c), a
 122+ A07C
 123+ A07C E1                   pop     hl
 124+ A07D D1                   pop     de
 125+ A07E C1                   pop     bc
 126+ A07F F1                   pop     af
 127+ A080 C9                   ret
 128+ A081
 129+ A081
 130+ A081              ; Reopen the permanent ATM shadow-port/DOSEN state after DS12887 access.
 131+ A081              ; #EFF7 bit 7 is the RTC gate; closing it also closes the permanent DOSEN
 132+ A081              ; path. Re-enter the TR-DOS #0177 service and restore RG6 text mode exactly
 133+ A081              ; as in Project126's atm_host implementation.
 134+ A081              p126_atm_restore_dosen:
 134+ A081
 135+ A081 F5                   push    af
 136+ A082 C5                   push    bc
 137+ A083 01 98 A0             ld      bc,p126_atm_restore_dosen_done
 138+ A086 C5                   push    bc
 139+ A087 01 53 2A             ld      bc,#2A53
 140+ A08A C5                   push    bc
 141+ A08B 01 77 01             ld      bc,#0177
 142+ A08E 3E 06                ld      a,P126_ATM_VIDEO_MODE_RG6_TEXT80X25
 143+ A090 32 00 A0             ld      (p126_atm_video_mode),a
 144+ A093 3E C6                ld      a,#C6
 145+ A095 C3 2F 3D             jp      #3D2F
 146+ A098              p126_atm_restore_dosen_done:
 147+ A098 C1                   pop     bc
 148+ A099 F1                   pop     af
 149+ A09A C9                   ret
 150+ A09B
 151+ A09B
 152+ A09B              p127_ui_init:

 156+ A0A4 CD 3E A1             call BIOS_DebugConsole_DrawInterface
 157+ A0A7 C9                   ret

 256+ A15F E1                   pop     hl
 257+ A160 C1                   pop     bc
 258+ A161 F1                   pop     af
 259+ A162 C9                   ret

 261+ A163              ; IN: HL=zero-terminated text, A=absolute row 0..24, C=column 0..79.
 262+ A163              ; Every static UI character is mirrored to port #EF.
 263+ A163              BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintAt:

 269+ A16A DA D3 A1             jp      c,.p126_print_at_denied
 270+ A16D                      ; The lease routine uses A; restore the complete caller frame before
 271+ A16D                      ; consuming the PrintAt row/column parameters.
 272+ A16D E1                   pop     hl
 273+ A16E D1                   pop     de
 274+ A16F C1                   pop     bc
 275+ A170 F1                   pop     af
 276+ A171 F5                   push    af
 277+ A172 C5                   push    bc
 278+ A173 D5                   push    de
 279+ A174 E5                   push    hl
 280+ A175 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 281+ A178 32 0F A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_row), a
 282+ A17B 79                   ld      a, c
 283+ A17C 32 10 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_print_col), a
 284+ A17F              .p126_print_at_loop:
 285+ A17F 2A 0D A0             ld      hl, (bios_dbg_text_ptr)
 286+ A182 7E                   ld      a, (hl)
 287+ A183 B7                   or      a
 288+ A184 CA C0 A1             jp      z, .p126_print_at_done
 289+ A187 32 06 A0             ld      (bios_dbg_char), a
 290+ A18A 23                   inc     hl
 291+ A18B 22 0D A0             ld      (bios_dbg_text_ptr), hl
 292+ A18E 3A 0F A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_row)
 293+ A191 57                   ld      d, a
 294+ A192 3A 10 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_print_col)
 295+ A195 5F                   ld      e, a
 296+ A196 3A 06 A0             ld      a, (bios_dbg_char)
 297+ A199 F5                   push    af
 298+ A19A 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT
 299+ A19D ED 79                out     (c),a
 300+ A19F F1                   pop     af
 301+ A1A0 CD 77 A2             call    p126_screen_write_char

 304+ A1A7 FE 50                cp      BIOS_DEBUG_COLUMNS

 315+ A1C0              .p126_print_at_done:

 317+ A1C3 01 EF 00             ld      bc,P127_UI_DEBUG_PORT

 321+ A1CC ED 79                out     (c),a

 323+ A1CF D1                   pop     de
 324+ A1D0 C1                   pop     bc
 325+ A1D1 F1                   pop     af

 334+ A1D8              ; IN: A=character. CR/LF/BS are handled; printable ASCII is rendered.

 600+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 601+ A40C              ; Project127 ATM video mode smoke test.
 602+ A40C              ; Reference mode bytes are taken from Project112's tested mode table:
 603+ A40C              ;   RG3 #C3 Sinclair, RG2 #C2 ATM640, RG0 #C0 EGA, RG6 #C6 text.
 604+ A40C              ; W1/W3 are remapped only while the UI owns W3. The trampoline is written
 605+ A40C              ; into the currently mapped video page 3 at #FFEC, then host pages are
 606+ A40C              ; restored immediately after the mode-register write.
 607+ A40C              ; ============================================================================
 608+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_SINCLAIR EQU #C3
 609+ A40C              P127_VIDEO_MODE_ATM640   EQU #C2

  93  A53A                      INCLUDE "src/kernel/p127_physmem.asm"
# file opened: p127_physmem.asm
   1+ A53A              ; ============================================================================
   2+ A53A              ; Project127 physical ATM RAM page test — Stage 2.23
   3+ A53A              ; Module version: 0.4.3
   4+ A53A              ; Runs from W2 (#A000..), with stack in W2. W1 resident/kernel remains fixed.
   5+ A53A              ; W3 is exclusively leased from the UI and restored to physical page 0.
   6+ A53A              ; This is the first general-purpose physical W3 remap test.
   7+ A53A              ; ============================================================================
   8+ A53A              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MAJOR EQU 0
   9+ A53A              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_MINOR EQU 4
  10+ A53A              P127_PHYSMEM_VERSION_PATCH EQU 3
  11+ A53A              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE     EQU 04000h
  12+ A53A              P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE   EQU 0C000h
  13+ A53A              P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_PORT     EQU 0FFF7h
  14+ A53A
  15+ A53A              ; OUT: carry=1 on any failure. Starts with two pages, then allocates and
  16+ A53A              ; scans every remaining free physical page under one W3 ownership lease.
  17+ A53A              ; The probe byte is complemented so it can never equal the saved P0 byte.
  18+ A53A              ; If the dispatcher is not actually switching, only that one P0 byte is
  19+ A53A              ; touched and it is restored before returning. Full-page writes start only
  20+ A53A              ; after the physical switch has been proven.
  21+ A53A              p127_physmem_batch_selftest:
  22+ A53A 3E 53                ld      a,'S'
  23+ A53C CD E8 AB             call    p127_physmem_trace
  24+ A53F AF                   xor     a
  25+ A540 32 AB AC             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
  26+ A543 32 AC AC             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  27+ A546 32 AD AC             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  28+ A549 32 AE AC             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  29+ A54C 32 AF AC             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  30+ A54F 32 B0 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  31+ A552
  32+ A552                      ; The host/staging page must be the documented W3 page before entry.
  33+ A552 3A 24 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
  34+ A555 B7                   or      a
  35+ A556 C2 ED A7             jp      nz,p127_physmem_fail_map_state
  36+ A559
  37+ A559 CD D0 74             call    p127_mem_alloc
  38+ A55C DA F2 A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  39+ A55F 32 B1 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_page_a),a
  40+ A562 32 B9 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_page_list),a
  41+ A565 3E 01                ld      a,1
  42+ A567 32 AC AC             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  43+ A56A
  44+ A56A CD D0 74             call    p127_mem_alloc
  45+ A56D DA F2 A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  46+ A570 32 B2 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_page_b),a
  47+ A573 32 BA AC             ld      (p127_physmem_page_list+1),a
  48+ A576 3E 02                ld      a,2
  49+ A578 32 AC AC             ld      (p127_physmem_alloc_count),a
  50+ A57B
  51+ A57B 3A B1 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
  52+ A57E 47                   ld      b,a
  53+ A57F 3A B2 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_b)
  54+ A582 B8                   cp      b
  55+ A583 CA F2 A7             jp      z,p127_physmem_fail_alloc
  56+ A586
  57+ A586                      ; No UI routine may run while W3 is reassigned.
  58+ A586 CD 9E 6F             call    p127_w3_quiesce
  59+ A589 DA F7 A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease
  60+ A58C 3E 01                ld      a,1
  61+ A58E 32 AD AC             ld      (p127_physmem_quiesced),a
  62+ A591 CD BD 6F             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
  63+ A594 DA F7 A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_lease
  64+ A597 3E 01                ld      a,1
  65+ A599 32 AE AC             ld      (p127_physmem_acquired),a
  66+ A59C
  67+ A59C                      ; Preserve one byte from P0 for a non-destructive port-open probe.
  68+ A59C 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  69+ A59F 32 B4 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_p0),a
  70+ A5A2 2F                   cpl
  71+ A5A3 32 B5 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_probe),a
  72+ A5A6 3E 01                ld      a,1
  73+ A5A8 32 B0 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_saved_valid),a
  74+ A5AB
  75+ A5AB                      ; Select allocated page A and write one complemented probe byte.
  76+ A5AB 3A B1 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
  77+ A5AE CD 6E AC             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
  78+ A5B1 DA FC A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
  79+ A5B4 3E 01                ld      a,1
  80+ A5B6 32 AF AC             ld      (p127_physmem_mapped),a
  81+ A5B9 3A B5 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_probe)
  82+ A5BC 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
  83+ A5BF
  84+ A5BF                      ; Return to P0. Its saved byte must be unchanged if the OUT worked.
  85+ A5BF AF                   xor     a
  86+ A5C0 CD 6E AC             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
  87+ A5C3 DA FC A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map

  90+ A5CA 3A B4 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_saved_p0)
  91+ A5CD B8                   cp      b
  92+ A5CE C2 01 A8             jp      nz,p127_physmem_fail_probe
  93+ A5D1                      ; Re-select page A and verify that its probe byte persisted.
  94+ A5D1 3A B1 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
  95+ A5D4 CD 6E AC             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
  96+ A5D7 DA FC A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
  97+ A5DA 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
  98+ A5DD 47                   ld      b,a
  99+ A5DE 3A B5 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_probe)
 100+ A5E1 B8                   cp      b
 101+ A5E2 C2 01 A8             jp      nz,p127_physmem_fail_probe

 103+ A5E5                      ; Stage 2.18: four address-sensitive pattern rounds. Every round
 104+ A5E5                      ; fills both pages, then alternates W3 and verifies retention.
 105+ A5E5 3A B1 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_page_a)
 106+ A5E8 CD 6E AC             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 107+ A5EB DA FC A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 108+ A5EE 16 11                ld      d,011h

 114+ A5FE CD 82 AC             call    p127_physmem_fill_pattern

 223+ A726
 224+ A726                      ; Stage 2.19: exhaust the allocator into a private page-ID list.
 225+ A726                      ; The first two pages are already listed. Each new allocation is
 226+ A726                      ; checked against all prior IDs before it is appended.
 227+ A726              p127_physmem_alloc_more:
 228+ A726 3A AC AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 229+ A729 FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 230+ A72B 30 2C                jr      nc,p127_physmem_alloc_complete
 231+ A72D CD D0 74             call    p127_mem_alloc
 232+ A730 38 27                jr      c,p127_physmem_alloc_complete
 233+ A732 32 B6 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_candidate),a
 234+ A735 57                   ld      d,a
 235+ A736 3A AC AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 236+ A739 47                   ld      b,a
 237+ A73A 21 B9 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 238+ A73D              p127_physmem_duplicate_scan:
 239+ A73D 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 240+ A73E BA                   cp      d
 241+ A73F CA 0B A8             jp      z,p127_physmem_fail_duplicate
 242+ A742 23                   inc     hl
 243+ A743 10 F8                djnz    p127_physmem_duplicate_scan

 245+ A748 5F                   ld      e,a

 255+ A759              p127_physmem_alloc_complete:
 256+ A759 3E 41                ld      a,'A'
 257+ A75B CD E8 AB             call    p127_physmem_trace
 258+ A75E                      ; Current map has exactly seven protected pages and 57 allocatable.
 259+ A75E 3A AC AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)

 263+ A769 DA 15 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_reserve
 264+ A76C
 265+ A76C                      ; First sweep: fill every allocated physical page with a unique
 266+ A76C                      ; address-derived pattern whose seed includes its physical page ID.
 267+ A76C 3A AC AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)

 269+ A772 21 B9 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 270+ A775              p127_physmem_full_fill_loop:
 271+ A775 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 272+ A776 23                   inc     hl
 273+ A777 32 B7 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_current_page),a
 274+ A77A 3E 46                ld      a,'F'

 277+ A782 CD 6E AC             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 278+ A785 DA FC A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map

 280+ A78B EE 5A                xor     05Ah
 281+ A78D 57                   ld      d,a
 282+ A78E E5                   push    hl
 283+ A78F CD 82 AC             call    p127_physmem_fill_pattern
 284+ A792 E1                   pop     hl
 285+ A793 3A B8 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 286+ A796 3D                   dec     a
 287+ A797 32 B8 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 288+ A79A 20 D9                jr      nz,p127_physmem_full_fill_loop
 289+ A79C
 290+ A79C 3E 46                ld      a,'F'
 291+ A79E CD E8 AB             call    p127_physmem_trace
 292+ A7A1
 293+ A7A1                      ; Reverse sweep verifies every page after all other pages have
 294+ A7A1                      ; already been written. This catches aliases and lost page contents.
 295+ A7A1 3A AC AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 296+ A7A4 5F                   ld      e,a
 297+ A7A5 16 00                ld      d,0
 298+ A7A7 21 B9 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 299+ A7AA 19                   add     hl,de
 300+ A7AB              p127_physmem_full_verify_loop:
 301+ A7AB 2B                   dec     hl
 302+ A7AC 7E                   ld      a,(hl)

 304+ A7B0 3E 56                ld      a,'V'

 307+ A7B8 CD 6E AC             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 308+ A7BB DA FC A7             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_map
 309+ A7BE 3A B7 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)

 312+ A7C4 E5                   push    hl

 315+ A7C9 DA 06 A8             jp      c,p127_physmem_fail_data
 316+ A7CC 3A B8 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 317+ A7CF                      ; Reinitialize the reverse counter on first entry.
 318+ A7CF B7                   or      a
 319+ A7D0 20 03                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready
 320+ A7D2 3A AC AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 321+ A7D5              p127_physmem_reverse_count_ready:
 322+ A7D5 3D                   dec     a
 323+ A7D6 32 B8 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a

 328+ A7E0
 329+ A7E0                      ; Verify the allocator still marks every listed page ALLOCATED.

 345+ A7FC              p127_physmem_fail_map:

 347+ A7FE C3 1C A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error
 348+ A801              p127_physmem_fail_probe:

 350+ A803 C3 1C A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error
 351+ A806              p127_physmem_fail_data:
 352+ A806 3E 06                ld      a,6
 353+ A808 C3 1C A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error
 354+ A80B              p127_physmem_fail_duplicate:
 355+ A80B 3E 07                ld      a,7
 356+ A80D C3 1C A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error
 357+ A810              p127_physmem_fail_count:

 359+ A812 C3 1C A8             jp      p127_physmem_set_error
 360+ A815              p127_physmem_fail_reserve:

 363+ A81A              p127_physmem_fail_state:

 367+ A81F
 368+ A81F              p127_physmem_cleanup:
 369+ A81F 3E 43                ld      a,'C'
 370+ A821 CD E8 AB             call    p127_physmem_trace
 371+ A824                      ; Always return W3 to P0 before releasing UI ownership.
 372+ A824 3A AF AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_mapped)
 373+ A827 B7                   or      a
 374+ A828 28 07                jr      z,p127_physmem_cleanup_saved
 375+ A82A AF                   xor     a
 376+ A82B CD 6E AC             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 377+ A82E DA E7 A9             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 378+ A831              p127_physmem_cleanup_saved:
 379+ A831 3A B0 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_saved_valid)
 380+ A834 B7                   or      a

 382+ A837 3A B4 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_saved_p0)
 383+ A83A 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 384+ A83D
 385+ A83D              p127_physmem_cleanup_release:
 386+ A83D 3A AE AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_acquired)

 388+ A841 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_cleanup_resume
 389+ A843 CD D1 6F             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 390+ A846 DA E7 A9             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 391+ A849              p127_physmem_cleanup_resume:
 392+ A849 3A AD AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_quiesced)
 393+ A84C B7                   or      a
 394+ A84D 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_cleanup_free
 395+ A84F CD E5 6F             call    p127_w3_resume
 396+ A852 DA E7 A9             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 397+ A855
 398+ A855              p127_physmem_cleanup_free:
 399+ A855 3A AC AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 400+ A858 32 B8 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 401+ A85B 3A AC AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 402+ A85E 5F                   ld      e,a
 403+ A85F 16 00                ld      d,0
 404+ A861 21 B9 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 405+ A864 19                   add     hl,de

 408+ A868 B7                   or      a
 409+ A869 28 13                jr      z,p127_physmem_cleanup_done
 410+ A86B 2B                   dec     hl

 413+ A86E CD E6 74             call    p127_mem_free
 414+ A871 E1                   pop     hl

 416+ A875 3A B8 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 417+ A878 3D                   dec     a
 418+ A879 32 B8 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 419+ A87C 18 E7                jr      p127_physmem_cleanup_free_loop
 420+ A87E              p127_physmem_cleanup_done:
 421+ A87E                      ; Reallocation must return the same lowest free page and release it.
 422+ A87E 3A AB AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)
 423+ A881 B7                   or      a
 424+ A882 20 21                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return
 425+ A884 CD D0 74             call    p127_mem_alloc
 426+ A887 DA E7 A9             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 427+ A88A 47                   ld      b,a

 433+ A896 DA E7 A9             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 434+ A899 CD EE A9             call    p127_physmem_check_reserved
 435+ A89C DA E7 A9             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 436+ A89F CD 4E AA             call    p127_physmem_check_free_state
 437+ A8A2 DA E7 A9             jp      c,p127_physmem_cleanup_error
 438+ A8A5              p127_physmem_cleanup_return:
 439+ A8A5 3A AB AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_error)
 440+ A8A8 B7                   or      a
 441+ A8A9 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error
 442+ A8AB                      ; The live page sweep ran with W3 exclusively leased, so progress was
 443+ A8AB                      ; mirrored to #EF only. Replay the compact page list now that W3=P0
 444+ A8AB                      ; and the UI owner has been restored.
 445+ A8AB CD B2 A8             call    p127_physmem_replay_page_log
 446+ A8AE B7                   or      a
 447+ A8AF C9                   ret
 448+ A8B0              p127_physmem_cleanup_return_error:
 449+ A8B0 37                   scf
 450+ A8B1 C9                   ret
 451+ A8B2
 452+ A8B2              ; Replay all successful physical-page selections on the visible ATM console.
 453+ A8B2              ; The 57 IDs are compacted into two wrapped lines rather than 114 long lines.
 454+ A8B2              p127_physmem_replay_page_log:
 455+ A8B2                      ; The W3 sweep and its cleanup may leave the video-bank selector in a
 456+ A8B2                      ; different state even though the CPU windows have been restored.
 457+ A8B2                      ; Reapply the proven Project126/ATM RG6 setup only after the W3 lease
 458+ A8B2                      ; is released and UI ownership is active. This preserves screen RAM.
 459+ A8B2 CD 11 A0             call    p126_atm_video_init

 461+ A8B5                      ; Fixed-row probe: if this label is visible, the selected alternate
 462+ A8B5                      ; text bank (P7 characters / P3 attributes) and PrintAt path agree.

 481+ A8DC 32 B8 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 482+ A8DF 20 ED                jr      nz,p127_physmem_replay_fill_loop

 486+ A8E8 CD D8 A1             call    BIOS_DebugConsole_PrintChar

 488+ A8EB 21 DB A9             ld      hl,p127_physmem_screen_verify_label
 489+ A8EE CD 33 A9             call    p127_physmem_screen_puts

 492+ A8F5 16 00                ld      d,0
 493+ A8F7 21 B9 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list

 503+ A90B 3A B8 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_scan_remaining)
 504+ A90E 3D                   dec     a
 505+ A90F 32 B8 AC             ld      (p127_physmem_scan_remaining),a
 506+ A912 20 ED                jr      nz,p127_physmem_replay_verify_loop

 512+ A91E                      ; Put the decisive result in the fixed status panel as well as the
 513+ A91E                      ; scrolling log. PrintAt addresses rows directly, so this does not
 514+ A91E                      ; depend on the current scrolling cursor after the long page replay.

 525+ A933              ; HL=zero-terminated string. The console routine preserves HL.

 528+ A934 B7                   or      a

 570+ A9E7              p127_physmem_cleanup_error:
 571+ A9E7 3E FF                ld      a,0FFh
 572+ A9E9 32 AB AC             ld      (p127_physmem_error),a
 573+ A9EC 37                   scf
 574+ A9ED C9                   ret
 575+ A9EE
 576+ A9EE              ; Check the seven fixed reservation states: P0/P1/P2/P3/P4/P5/P7.
 577+ A9EE              ; OUT carry=1 if any protected page is not RESERVED.
 578+ A9EE              p127_physmem_check_reserved:
 579+ A9EE 21 30 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 580+ A9F1 06 08                ld      b,8
 581+ A9F3 0E 00                ld      c,0
 582+ A9F5              p127_physmem_reserved_loop:
 583+ A9F5 79                   ld      a,c
 584+ A9F6 FE 06                cp      6
 585+ A9F8 28 2E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 586+ A9FA FE 07                cp      7
 587+ A9FC 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 588+ A9FE FE 00                cp      0
 589+ AA00 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 590+ AA02 FE 01                cp      1
 591+ AA04 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 592+ AA06 FE 02                cp      2
 593+ AA08 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 594+ AA0A FE 03                cp      3
 595+ AA0C 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 596+ AA0E FE 04                cp      4
 597+ AA10 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 598+ AA12 FE 05                cp      5
 599+ AA14 28 02                jr      z,p127_physmem_reserved_check
 600+ AA16 18 10                jr      p127_physmem_reserved_skip
 601+ AA18              p127_physmem_reserved_check:
 602+ AA18 C5                   push    bc
 603+ AA19 E5                   push    hl
 604+ AA1A 59                   ld      e,c
 605+ AA1B 16 00                ld      d,0
 606+ AA1D 21 30 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 607+ AA20 19                   add     hl,de
 608+ AA21 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 609+ AA22 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 610+ AA24 E1                   pop     hl
 611+ AA25 C1                   pop     bc
 612+ AA26 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_reserved_fail
 613+ AA28              p127_physmem_reserved_skip:
 614+ AA28 0C                   inc     c
 615+ AA29 10 CA                djnz    p127_physmem_reserved_loop
 616+ AA2B B7                   or      a
 617+ AA2C C9                   ret
 618+ AA2D              p127_physmem_reserved_fail:
 619+ AA2D 37                   scf
 620+ AA2E C9                   ret
 621+ AA2F
 622+ AA2F              ; Every page in the private list must remain ALLOCATED during the sweep.
 623+ AA2F              p127_physmem_check_allocated:
 624+ AA2F 3A AC AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_alloc_count)
 625+ AA32 47                   ld      b,a
 626+ AA33 21 B9 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_page_list
 627+ AA36              p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop:
 628+ AA36 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 629+ AA37 5F                   ld      e,a
 630+ AA38 16 00                ld      d,0
 631+ AA3A C5                   push    bc
 632+ AA3B E5                   push    hl
 633+ AA3C 21 30 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 634+ AA3F 19                   add     hl,de
 635+ AA40 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 636+ AA41 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 637+ AA43 E1                   pop     hl
 638+ AA44 C1                   pop     bc
 639+ AA45 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail
 640+ AA47 23                   inc     hl
 641+ AA48 10 EC                djnz    p127_physmem_alloc_state_loop
 642+ AA4A B7                   or      a
 643+ AA4B C9                   ret
 644+ AA4C              p127_physmem_alloc_state_fail:
 645+ AA4C 37                   scf
 646+ AA4D C9                   ret
 647+ AA4E
 648+ AA4E              ; After release, every non-reserved page must be FREE; reserved pages
 649+ AA4E              ; are checked separately by p127_physmem_check_reserved.
 650+ AA4E              p127_physmem_check_free_state:
 651+ AA4E 21 30 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 652+ AA51 06 40                ld      b,64
 653+ AA53 0E 00                ld      c,0
 654+ AA55              p127_physmem_free_state_loop:
 655+ AA55 79                   ld      a,c
 656+ AA56 FE 00                cp      0
 657+ AA58 28 1E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 658+ AA5A FE 01                cp      1
 659+ AA5C 28 1A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 660+ AA5E FE 02                cp      2
 661+ AA60 28 16                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 662+ AA62 FE 03                cp      3
 663+ AA64 28 12                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 664+ AA66 FE 04                cp      4
 665+ AA68 28 0E                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 666+ AA6A FE 05                cp      5
 667+ AA6C 28 0A                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 668+ AA6E FE 07                cp      7
 669+ AA70 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_free_state_reserved
 670+ AA72 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 671+ AA73 B7                   or      a
 672+ AA74 20 0D                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 673+ AA76 18 05                jr      p127_physmem_free_state_next
 674+ AA78              p127_physmem_free_state_reserved:
 675+ AA78 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 676+ AA79 FE 01                cp      P127_MEM_RESERVED
 677+ AA7B 20 06                jr      nz,p127_physmem_free_state_fail
 678+ AA7D              p127_physmem_free_state_next:
 679+ AA7D 23                   inc     hl
 680+ AA7E 0C                   inc     c
 681+ AA7F 10 D4                djnz    p127_physmem_free_state_loop
 682+ AA81 B7                   or      a
 683+ AA82 C9                   ret
 684+ AA83              p127_physmem_free_state_fail:
 685+ AA83 37                   scf
 686+ AA84 C9                   ret
 687+ AA85
 688+ AA85              ; Emit one printable phase marker directly on the established #EF debug port.
 689+ AA85              ; This remains available while the UI is quiesced and W3 is leased.
 690+ AA85
 691+ AA85              ; ============================================================================
 692+ AA85              ; Stage 2.20 — guarded physical-page API.
 693+ AA85              ; All code and stack remain in W2. The only hardware mapping operation is W3.
 694+ AA85              ; Public ABI:
 695+ AA85              ;   alloc_page: OUT A=allocated page, carry=1 if exhausted.
 696+ AA85              ;   free_page:  IN A=allocated page, carry=1 if invalid or lease active.
 697+ AA85              ;   w3_begin:  quiesce UI, acquire W3, save P0 byte; DI remains in force.
 698+ AA85              ;   w3_map:    IN A=allocated page; valid only inside an active W3 lease.
 699+ AA85              ;   w3_end:    restore W3=P0 and saved byte, release lease, resume UI.
 700+ AA85              ; No API maps W0/W1/W2, frees a currently mapped page, or enables IRQs.
 701+ AA85              ; ============================================================================
 702+ AA85              p127_physmem_alloc_page:
 703+ AA85 C3 D0 74             jp      p127_mem_alloc
 704+ AA88
 705+ AA88              p127_physmem_free_page:
 706+ AA88 32 E5 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 707+ AA8B 3A E0 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 708+ AA8E B7                   or      a
 709+ AA8F C2 42 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 710+ AA92 3A E5 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 711+ AA95 47                   ld      b,a
 712+ AA96 3A 24 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 713+ AA99 B8                   cp      b
 714+ AA9A CA 42 AB             jp      z,p127_physmem_api_reject
 715+ AA9D 3A E5 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 716+ AAA0 C3 E6 74             jp      p127_mem_free
 717+ AAA3
 718+ AAA3              ; Begin a bounded W3 lease. Bootstrap mapping must be W3=P0.
 719+ AAA3              p127_physmem_w3_begin:
 720+ AAA3 F3                   di
 721+ AAA4 3A E0 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 722+ AAA7 B7                   or      a
 723+ AAA8 C2 42 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 724+ AAAB 3A 24 86             ld      a,(p127_map_shadow+3)
 725+ AAAE B7                   or      a
 726+ AAAF C2 42 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 727+ AAB2 CD 9E 6F             call    p127_w3_quiesce
 728+ AAB5 D8                   ret     c
 729+ AAB6 3E 01                ld      a,1
 730+ AAB8 32 E1 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 731+ AABB CD BD 6F             call    p127_w3_handoff_acquire
 732+ AABE 38 15                jr      c,p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback
 733+ AAC0 3E 01                ld      a,1
 734+ AAC2 32 E2 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 735+ AAC5 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 736+ AAC8 32 E4 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_p0),a
 737+ AACB 3E 01                ld      a,1
 738+ AACD 32 E3 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 739+ AAD0 32 E0 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 740+ AAD3 B7                   or      a
 741+ AAD4 C9                   ret
 742+ AAD5              p127_physmem_w3_begin_rollback:
 743+ AAD5 CD E5 6F             call    p127_w3_resume
 744+ AAD8 AF                   xor     a
 745+ AAD9 32 E1 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 746+ AADC 37                   scf
 747+ AADD C9                   ret
 748+ AADE
 749+ AADE              ; Map only an allocator-owned page into W3.
 750+ AADE              p127_physmem_w3_map:
 751+ AADE 32 E5 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_page_arg),a
 752+ AAE1 3A E0 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 753+ AAE4 FE 01                cp      1
 754+ AAE6 C2 42 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 755+ AAE9 3A E5 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 756+ AAEC FE 40                cp      P127_PHYSMEM_MAX_PAGES
 757+ AAEE D2 42 AB             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_reject
 758+ AAF1 5F                   ld      e,a
 759+ AAF2 16 00                ld      d,0
 760+ AAF4 21 30 77             ld      hl,p127_mem_page_state
 761+ AAF7 19                   add     hl,de
 762+ AAF8 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 763+ AAF9 FE 02                cp      P127_MEM_ALLOCATED
 764+ AAFB C2 42 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 765+ AAFE 3A E5 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_page_arg)
 766+ AB01 C3 6E AC             jp      p127_physmem_set_w3
 767+ AB04
 768+ AB04              ; End the lease in recovery order. If release/resume fails, state flags are
 769+ AB04              ; retained so the caller can retry this routine without losing ownership.
 770+ AB04              p127_physmem_w3_end:
 771+ AB04 3A E0 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_active)
 772+ AB07 FE 01                cp      1
 773+ AB09 C2 42 AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_reject
 774+ AB0C AF                   xor     a
 775+ AB0D CD 6E AC             call    p127_physmem_set_w3
 776+ AB10 D8                   ret     c
 777+ AB11 3A E3 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_valid)
 778+ AB14 B7                   or      a
 779+ AB15 28 06                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_release
 780+ AB17 3A E4 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_saved_p0)
 781+ AB1A 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 782+ AB1D              p127_physmem_w3_end_release:
 783+ AB1D 3A E2 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_acquired)
 784+ AB20 B7                   or      a
 785+ AB21 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_resume
 786+ AB23 CD D1 6F             call    p127_w3_handoff_release
 787+ AB26 D8                   ret     c
 788+ AB27 AF                   xor     a
 789+ AB28 32 E2 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 790+ AB2B              p127_physmem_w3_end_resume:
 791+ AB2B 3A E1 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_quiesced)
 792+ AB2E B7                   or      a
 793+ AB2F 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_w3_end_done
 794+ AB31 CD E5 6F             call    p127_w3_resume
 795+ AB34 D8                   ret     c
 796+ AB35 AF                   xor     a
 797+ AB36 32 E1 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 798+ AB39              p127_physmem_w3_end_done:
 799+ AB39 AF                   xor     a
 800+ AB3A 32 E3 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 801+ AB3D 32 E0 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 802+ AB40 B7                   or      a
 803+ AB41 C9                   ret
 804+ AB42
 805+ AB42              p127_physmem_api_reject:
 806+ AB42 37                   scf
 807+ AB43 C9                   ret
 808+ AB44
 809+ AB44              ; Runtime API contract test. It proves rejection paths, allocated-page-only
 810+ AB44              ; mapping, mapped-page free protection, P0 byte restoration and UI recovery.
 811+ AB44              p127_physmem_api_selftest:
 812+ AB44 AF                   xor     a
 813+ AB45 32 E0 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_active),a
 814+ AB48 32 E1 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_quiesced),a
 815+ AB4B 32 E2 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_acquired),a
 816+ AB4E 32 E3 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_saved_valid),a
 817+ AB51 3E 01                ld      a,1
 818+ AB53 CD 88 AA             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 819+ AB56 D2 DE AB             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 820+ AB59 CD 85 AA             call    p127_physmem_alloc_page
 821+ AB5C DA DE AB             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 822+ AB5F 32 E6 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_page),a
 823+ AB62 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 824+ AB65 32 E7 AB             ld      (p127_physmem_api_test_p0),a
 825+ AB68 CD A3 AA             call    p127_physmem_w3_begin
 826+ AB6B DA D8 AB             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_free
 827+ AB6E                      ; A reserved page must never be accepted by the map API.
 828+ AB6E 3E 01                ld      a,1
 829+ AB70 CD DE AA             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 830+ AB73 D2 D5 AB             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 831+ AB76 3A E6 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 832+ AB79 CD DE AA             call    p127_physmem_w3_map
 833+ AB7C DA D5 AB             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 834+ AB7F                      ; The currently mapped page cannot be freed while the lease is held.
 835+ AB7F 3A E6 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)
 836+ AB82 CD 88 AA             call    p127_physmem_free_page
 837+ AB85 D2 D5 AB             jp      nc,p127_physmem_api_test_end
 838+ AB88 3E A5                ld      a,0A5h
 839+ AB8A 32 00 C0             ld      (P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE),a
 840+ AB8D CD 04 AB             call    p127_physmem_w3_end
 841+ AB90 DA DE AB             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 842+ AB93 3A 00 C0             ld      a,(P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE)
 843+ AB96 47                   ld      b,a
 844+ AB97 3A E7 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_p0)
 845+ AB9A B8                   cp      b
 846+ AB9B C2 DE AB             jp      nz,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
 847+ AB9E 3A E6 AB             ld      a,(p127_physmem_api_test_page)

 849+ ABA4 DA DE AB             jp      c,p127_physmem_api_test_fail
