 891+ ABE8 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 892+ ABEB ED 79                out     (c),a
 893+ ABED C9                   ret
 894+ ABEE
 895+ ABEE              ; Emit progress for each physical page while the UI is quiesced.
 896+ ABEE              ; A='F' prints FILL PAGE ID=xx; A='V' prints VERIFY PAGE ID=xx.
 897+ ABEE              ; Preserve every register because the caller owns the page-list cursor.
 898+ ABEE              p127_physmem_trace_page:
 899+ ABEE F5                   push    af
 900+ ABEF C5                   push    bc
 901+ ABF0 D5                   push    de
 902+ ABF1 E5                   push    hl
 903+ ABF2 FE 46                cp      'F'
 904+ ABF4 20 05                jr      nz,p127_physmem_trace_page_verify
 905+ ABF6 21 40 AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_fill_text
 906+ ABF9 18 03                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 907+ ABFB              p127_physmem_trace_page_verify:
 908+ ABFB 21 4E AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_verify_text
 909+ ABFE              p127_physmem_trace_page_text:
 910+ ABFE 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 911+ ABFF B7                   or      a
 912+ AC00 28 08                jr      z,p127_physmem_trace_page_value
 913+ AC02 23                   inc     hl
 914+ AC03 E5                   push    hl
 915+ AC04 CD 3A AC             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 916+ AC07 E1                   pop     hl
 917+ AC08 18 F4                jr      p127_physmem_trace_page_text
 918+ AC0A              p127_physmem_trace_page_value:
 919+ AC0A 3A B7 AC             ld      a,(p127_physmem_current_page)
 920+ AC0D CD 1F AC             call    p127_physmem_trace_hex8
 921+ AC10 3E 0D                ld      a,13
 922+ AC12 CD 3A AC             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 923+ AC15 3E 0A                ld      a,10
 924+ AC17 CD 3A AC             call    p127_physmem_trace_char
 925+ AC1A E1                   pop     hl
 926+ AC1B D1                   pop     de
 927+ AC1C C1                   pop     bc
 928+ AC1D F1                   pop     af
 929+ AC1E C9                   ret
 930+ AC1F
 931+ AC1F              p127_physmem_trace_hex8:
 932+ AC1F F5                   push    af
 933+ AC20 E6 F0                and     0F0h
 934+ AC22 0F                   rrca
 935+ AC23 0F                   rrca
 936+ AC24 0F                   rrca
 937+ AC25 0F                   rrca
 938+ AC26 CD 2F AC             call    p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 939+ AC29 F1                   pop     af
 940+ AC2A E6 0F                and     00Fh
 941+ AC2C C3 2F AC             jp      p127_physmem_trace_nibble
 942+ AC2F
 943+ AC2F              p127_physmem_trace_nibble:
 944+ AC2F 5F                   ld      e,a
 945+ AC30 16 00                ld      d,0
 946+ AC32 21 5E AC             ld      hl,p127_physmem_trace_hex_table
 947+ AC35 19                   add     hl,de
 948+ AC36 7E                   ld      a,(hl)
 949+ AC37 C3 3A AC             jp      p127_physmem_trace_char
 950+ AC3A
 951+ AC3A              p127_physmem_trace_char:
 952+ AC3A 01 EF 00             ld      bc,00EFh
 953+ AC3D ED 79                out     (c),a
 954+ AC3F C9                   ret

 962+ AC6E
 963+ AC6E              ; IN A=physical RAM page ID. Program only W3 (#FFF7), never W0/W1/W2.
 964+ AC6E              ; D6=1 selects RAM, D7=0 selects the inverse D0-D5 page ID.
 965+ AC6E              p127_physmem_set_w3:

 967+ AC71 CD 41 84             call    p127_map_encode_page

 973+ AC80 B7                   or      a
 974+ AC81 C9                   ret
 975+ AC82
 976+ AC82              ; IN D=pattern seed. Byte(address)=ROL(address_low) XOR address_high XOR D.
 977+ AC82              ; The same address produces a different expected value at each seed.
 978+ AC82              p127_physmem_fill_pattern:
 979+ AC82 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 980+ AC85 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 981+ AC88              .p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop:
 982+ AC88 7D                   ld      a,l
 983+ AC89 07                   rlca
 984+ AC8A AC                   xor     h
 985+ AC8B AA                   xor     d
 986+ AC8C 77                   ld      (hl),a
 987+ AC8D 23                   inc     hl
 988+ AC8E 0B                   dec     bc
 989+ AC8F 78                   ld      a,b
 990+ AC90 B1                   or      c
 991+ AC91 20 F5                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_fill_pattern_loop
 992+ AC93 C9                   ret
 993+ AC94
 994+ AC94              ; IN D=pattern seed. Verify all 16 KiB against the address-derived pattern.
 995+ AC94              p127_physmem_verify_pattern:
 996+ AC94 21 00 C0             ld      hl,P127_PHYSMEM_WINDOW_BASE
 997+ AC97 01 00 40             ld      bc,P127_PHYSMEM_PAGE_SIZE
 998+ AC9A              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop:
 999+ AC9A 7D                   ld      a,l
1000+ AC9B 07                   rlca
1001+ AC9C AC                   xor     h
1002+ AC9D AA                   xor     d
1003+ AC9E BE                   cp      (hl)
1004+ AC9F 20 08                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail
1005+ ACA1 23                   inc     hl
1006+ ACA2 0B                   dec     bc
1007+ ACA3 78                   ld      a,b
1008+ ACA4 B1                   or      c
1009+ ACA5 20 F3                jr      nz,.p127_physmem_verify_pattern_loop
1010+ ACA7 B7                   or      a
1011+ ACA8 C9                   ret
1012+ ACA9              .p127_physmem_verify_pattern_fail:
